User: Fill in the blank: 17 - 3 = ?
Assistant: 17 - 3 = 14.

User: A box holds 17 beads. I remove 3. How many beads remain?
Assistant: There are 14 beads left. 17 minus 3 is 14.

User: Start at 17 and count backward 3 steps. Where do you land?
Assistant: You land on 14. 17 minus 3 is 14.

User: Share 40 beads equally among 4 cups. How many beads go in each cup?
Assistant: Each cup gets 10 beads. 40 divided by 4 is 10.

User: How many groups of 4 can you make from 40?
Assistant: You can make 10 groups. 40 divided by 4 is 10.

User: Fill in the blank: 20 - 16 = ?
Assistant: 20 - 16 = 4.

User: A box holds 20 beads. I remove 16. How many beads remain?
Assistant: There are 4 beads left. 20 minus 16 is 4.

User: Start at 20 and count backward 16 steps. Where do you land?
Assistant: You land on 4. 20 minus 16 is 4.

User: What is 56 / 7?
Assistant: 56 divided by 7 is 8.

Assistant: 56 divided by 7 is 8.

User: Fill in the blank: 56 / 7 = ?
Assistant: 56 / 7 = 8.

User: Share 56 beads equally among 7 cups. How many beads go in each cup?
Assistant: Each cup gets 8 beads. 56 divided by 7 is 8.

User: How many groups of 7 can you make from 56?
Assistant: You can make 8 groups. 56 divided by 7 is 8.
